# Awesome Tornado AI — source-linked catalog

Generated from `data/papers.json`. Entries distinguish method annotations from bibliography-only indexing. This is not an exhaustive systematic review. No full paper abstracts are reproduced.

## 01 · Foundations & dynamics

<a id="kieu2026"></a>
### 2026 · A note on the shear-forced dynamics of tornadoes

Chanh Kieu · *arXiv* · **preprint** · `direct` · `annotated`

Derives an idealized shear-forced vortex solution and studies how internal vertical shear changes the radius and decay of the rotating core.

推导受内部垂直切变作用的理想化涡旋解，讨论切变如何改变旋转核心的半径、垂直结构和衰减。

- **Method / input:** Axisymmetric viscous vortex theory / Prescribed incompressible circulation and internal vertical wind shear.
- **Target & horizon:** Structural change and decay of an idealized tornado-like vortex.
- **Reading caution:** Preprint; the prescribed axisymmetric flow is a mechanism study, not an operational tornado lifetime forecast. Metadata/abstract-level review; experiments not independently reproduced.
- **Provenance:** metadata checked 2026-09-29; independently reproduced: false.

[Primary source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00416) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00416)

Code availability: not verified in this snapshot.

<a id="bradshaw2026"></a>
### 2026 · Singular stationary Navier-Stokes flows: examples and stability

Zachary Bradshaw; Dakota Palmer · *arXiv* · **preprint** · `context` · `annotated`

Studies singular steady flows and their stability, including swirling-vortex examples with qualitative features resembling tornado circulation.

研究有边界区域中奇异定常流的结构与稳定性，其中旋转涡流示例与龙卷环流具有部分定性相似特征。

- **Method / input:** Analysis of singular stationary Navier–Stokes flows / Idealized steady flows in domains with boundaries.
- **Target & horizon:** Structure and stability of mathematical vortices related to Serrin swirling flows.
- **Reading caution:** Preprint; qualitative tornado-like structure is physical context, not validation against observed atmospheric tornadoes. Metadata/abstract-level review; experiments not independently reproduced.
- **Provenance:** metadata checked 2026-09-29; independently reproduced: false.

[Primary source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22291) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22291)

Code availability: not verified in this snapshot.

Robert Davies-Jones · *Journal of the Atmospheric Sciences* · **journal** · `context` · `annotated`

Relates streamwise environmental vorticity to rotation within a convective updraft, establishing a physical basis for storm-relative helicity reasoning.

<a id="fujita1981"></a>
### 1981 · Tornadoes and Downbursts in the Context of Generalized Planetary Scales

T. Theodore Fujita · *Journal of the Atmospheric Sciences* · **journal** · `direct` · `annotated`

Places tornadoes and downbursts in a hierarchy of atmospheric motion scales using storm analysis and aerial evidence of wind effects.

结合风暴分析和航空灾损照片讨论龙卷、下击暴流及其母体系统的尺度关系，帮助区分不同强风现象。

- **Method / input:** Scale classification and observational synthesis / Storm analyses and aerial damage photography.
- **Target & horizon:** Classification of tornado and downburst flow scales; retrospective.

<a id="browning1963"></a>
### 1963 · Airflow and Structure of a Tornadic Storm

K. A. Browning; R. J. Donaldson · *Journal of the Atmospheric Sciences* · **journal** · `direct` · `annotated`

Reconstructs airflow and storm structure to connect the parent thunderstorm with the tornado, providing a physical interpretation of observed radar morphology.

通过风暴观测重建气流与结构，将龙卷放回母体雷暴中理解，为解释雷达回波形态提供物理背景。

- **Method / input:** Storm airflow reconstruction / Radar and storm observations.
- **Target & horizon:** Tornadic-storm structure; retrospective.
- **Reading caution:** A storm-structure case study is not an automated detection benchmark. Metadata/abstract-level review; experiments not independently reproduced.
- **Provenance:** metadata checked 2026-09-29; independently reproduced: false.

## 08 · Datasets & benchmarks

<a id="chen2026xwod"></a>
### 2026 · XWOD: A Real-World Benchmark for Object Detection under Extreme Weather Conditions

Chih-Hsin Chen; Yu-Tung Liu; Amar Fadillah; Kuan-Ting Lai; Dong Liu · *arXiv* · **preprint** · `context` · `annotated`

Introduces a traffic-object benchmark whose weather taxonomy includes tornado scenes, useful for separating weather robustness from tornado recognition.

发布包含龙卷场景标签的恶劣天气交通目标检测基准，可用于研究灾害环境中的视觉鲁棒性。

- **Method / input:** Extreme-weather traffic-object detection benchmark / Real-world traffic images labeled with weather conditions, including tornadoes.
- **Target & horizon:** Detection of road users and vehicles in adverse-weather imagery.
- **Reading caution:** Preprint; targets are traffic objects, not tornado boxes, radar signatures or EF intensity labels. Aggregate transfer results are not tornado detection accuracy. Metadata/abstract-level review; experiments not independently reproduced.
- **Provenance:** metadata checked 2026-09-29; independently reproduced: false.

[Primary source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11521) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11521)

Code availability: not verified in this snapshot.

<a id="tornet2025"></a>
### 2025 · A Benchmark Dataset for Tornado Detection and Prediction using Full-Resolution Polarimetric Weather Radar Data

Mark S. Veillette; James M. Kurdzo; Phillip M. Stepanian; John Y. N. Cho; Tony Reis; Siddharth Samsi; Joseph McDonald; Nicholas Chisler · *Artificial Intelligence for the Earth Systems* · **journal** · `direct` · `annotated`

TorNet provides an open radar benchmark and baseline from MIT Lincoln Laboratory.

MIT Lincoln Laboratory 的开放雷达数据集与基线；为可复现的龙卷检测研究提供入口。

- **Method / input:** Dataset and CNN baseline / Full-resolution polarimetric weather radar.
- **Target & horizon:** Tornado detection benchmark; prediction extensions.
- **Reading caution:** TorNet belongs to MIT Lincoln Laboratory, not TornadoLab. Six radar fields are six variables, not six independent sensing modalities. The original benchmark baseline is chiefly detection; a forecasting claim needs explicit causal lead-time evaluation.
- **Provenance:** metadata checked 2026-09-29; independently reproduced: false.

[Primary source](https://journals.ametsoc.org/view/journals/aies/4/1/AIES-D-24-0006.1.xml) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1175/AIES-D-24-0006.1) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16437) · [Author-linked code](https://github.com/mit-ll/tornet) · [Evidence 2](https://arxiv.org/html/2401.16437v1) · [Evidence 3](https://github.com/mit-ll/tornet) · [Evidence 4](https://api.crossref.org/works/10.1175%2Faies-d-24-0006.1)
